## 06.49-01

- The counts in the `fails_when` clauses match source:
  - Navbar: 3×2×5×2×2 = 120 cells, 80 of them Guardian or mixed (`tests/test1/test_navbar_costs.py:91-95`).
  - Child cases: the `-k` selection gives 2+2+1 = 5 (`tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1134`, `:1291`, `:1320`).
  - Ancestor slices: 4 matrix + 4 `-list-with-create-button` + 1 `top-level` = 9 (`tests/test1/test_ancestor_costs.py:204-208`, `:263-266`, `:317`).
- The outer-SELECT classifier the task reuses exists (`tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1034-1056`).

**Concerns.** None.

**Suggestions.** None required.

**Risk.** LOW. The remaining risk is execution discipline (measure before pinning), which the acceptance criteria and the NEW-1 gate already enforce.

## 06.49-02

**Summary.** Adds `_get_guardian_checker(user)`, replaces the four constructions, keeps `_request_checker` as a stateless delegate, and adds isolation, anonymous and F-01 tests. Unchanged since cycle 4.

**Strengths**

- The F-01 exemption targets exactly the two comprehensions at `tests/test1/test_link_access.py:469` and `:476`; the dynamic gate/own/state loop at `:478-498` is untouched.
- H-02 is grounded: the suite sets `ANONYMOUS_USER_NAME=None` (`tests/test1/conftest.py:128`), and the cited precedent patches Guardian's own settings object (`tests/test1/test_delete_databases.py:32`, `:42`).

**Concerns.** None actionable. One point considered and dismissed: Guardian's grant cache key is (content type, pk) per RESEARCH. That carries no alias, but the per-call grant query was never bound to the object's alias either, so a shared checker changes only timing, which D-37 settles. Nested paths stay pinned to `default` (`mixins.py:176`, `:199`).

**Suggestions.** None required.

**Risk.** LOW to MEDIUM during waves 2–3, because the tree is knowingly red for F-02 to F-06 and the cost pins. The plan names that inventory and it matches the probe's 101.

## Cross-plan analysis

### NEW-2 — CLOSED (planning closure, not execution certification)

I accept the selected disposition: document the recorder-enabled pass as unexecuted and uncertified. It was one of the two closures offered in cycle 4.

- **The plan carries it in every required place.** The truth, the 05-03 action, the acceptance criterion, `<read_first>` (adds `tests/conftest.py`), the SUMMARY obligation in `<output>`, and both Round 4 ledger rows anchored to `0ca3d58b` all name it. `06.49-VALIDATION.md` mirrors it as a limitation paragraph and a sign-off checkbox.
- **Its factual claims are correct.** nox reruns the suite with `CV_ACCESS_RECORD_REQUESTS=1` (`noxfile.py:49-57`) after a first pass with `0` (`noxfile.py:30`). The autouse fixture enters a recorder per test and clears it (`tests/conftest.py:8-21`).
- **Nothing in source contradicts the deferral.**
  - The recorder adds no SQL: `record()` builds a dataclass and appends (`src/crud_views/lib/access_trace.py:67-85`); `capture_filter` keeps unevaluated querysets (`:87-90`); `_object_identity` reads only the pk and model metadata (`:48-56`).
  - It writes nothing on the user object, so neither the `_cv_guardian_checker` attribute nor the F-01 attribute check can behave differently under it.
  - The existing exact-pin tests already run under both passes in CI through the same record calls (`mixins.py:157-170`, `ancestors.py:91-104`).
- **Limit of this closure.** Whether the phase's new tests pass in recorder mode remains unknown until CI runs. The plan says so and must report it.

## 06.49-01

### Summary
Plan `.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-01-PLAN.md` (Wave 1, 2 tasks: `01-01`, `01-02`) establishes the mandatory pre-change measurement baseline before any production edit to checker construction. It introduces `tests/test1/test_guardian_checker_costs.py::test_guardian_list_row_action_cost` against `GuardianPublisherListView` (`tests/test1/app/views.py:709-739`) for `row_count=[0, 1, 3]` and preserves starting-tree receipts in `.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-COST-BASELINE.md` for both isolated and combined four-module runs alongside the 120-cell navbar matrix (`tests/test1/test_navbar_costs.py:90-183`), ancestor slices (`tests/test1/test_ancestor_costs.py:181-200`), and the five F-03 child-link query cases (`tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1133-1158, 1290-1371`).

### Strengths
- **True pre-change baseline against an existing Guardian list view:** `.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-01-PLAN.md:59, 67-68` targets the existing `cv_guardian_publisher` and `GuardianPublisherListView` (`tests/test1/app/views.py:709-720`, with `cv_list_actions = ["detail", "update", "delete"]` and sibling views at `tests/test1/app/views.py:722-739`) while all four `ObjectPermissionChecker` construction sites (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:153, 210` and `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/ancestors.py:67, 70`) remain untouched.
- **Explicit cache reset-then-warm protocol and isolated-vs-combined agreement (NEW-1):** `.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-01-PLAN.md:89, 103-104` reuses the cache-reset pattern from `tests/test1/test_navbar_costs.py:34-69` (`cache_conditions`) so process-global `ContentType` or permission vocabulary state left by other modules cannot skew `row_count=[0, 1, 3]` pins between serial (`01-01`) and six-worker combined (`01-02`) runs.
- **SQL category separation and HTML action verification:** `.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-01-PLAN.md:67-68` separates outer checker grant `SELECT`s from `get_objects_for_user` list-filter subqueries (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:337-359`) and verifies in rendered HTML that `detail` is present while `update`/`delete` and ungranted rows are absent.

### Concerns
None (no HIGH, MEDIUM, or LOW concerns).

### Suggestions
None.

### Risk
Low. Pre-change measurements are isolated to a new test module and a planning baseline document, with explicit verification that production files have zero diff before Wave 2 begins.

### Strengths
- **Exact symbol-grounded replacement of all four construction sites (D-02, F-08):** `.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-02-PLAN.md:80-82` replaces `ObjectPermissionChecker(user)` in `GuardianObjectPermissionMixin._check_object_perm` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:151-154`) and `GuardianObjectPermissionMixin.cv_has_access` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:208-211`), and turns `ancestors._request_checker` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/ancestors.py:61-72`) into a thin delegate calling `_get_guardian_checker(user)` while removing `view._cv_guardian_ancestor_checker` and updating the module docstring (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/ancestors.py:21-23`).

Actor admission, inactive-user denial (`_guardian_inactive_actor`), `_check_nested_auth`, model-level fallback, and `cv_action_enabled` ordering remain untouched in `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:141-212` and `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/ancestors.py:75-105`.

### Concerns
None (no HIGH, MEDIUM, or LOW concerns).

### Suggestions
None.

Low. The production change is bounded to one private helper and four call sites, with immediate verification against `test_access_trace_guardian.py`, `test_guardian_inactive.py`, `test_no_decision_is_stored`, and the frozen 1,178-case `test_link_access_matrix.py`.

## Cross-Plan Analysis & Cycle 5 Prior-Finding Closure Review

### Wave Ordering, Scope, Security, and Goal Achievement
- **Structure and sequencing:** Five sequential waves (`06.49-01` through `06.49-05`) with 11 tasks (`2 + 2 + 2 + 2 + 3`) have clean data and file-ownership handoffs: Wave 1 pins immutable before receipts on unchanged production; Wave 2 lands `_get_guardian_checker(user)` and core decision-isolation/anonymous regressions; Wave 3 lands D-37 workflow/lifetime and F-05/F-06 test-shape adaptations; Wave 4 reconciles post-change query pins and child-table receipts; Wave 5 updates reference/migration/changelog docs and closes multi-series regression and validation evidence.
- **Maintainability and scope (D-01, D-09):** The production change is confined to `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/permissions.py`, `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py`, and `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/ancestors.py`. No cross-request cache, decision cache, `prefetch_perms`, link-check redesign, or `### Breaking` change is introduced.
- **Security (ASVS 4.0.3 L1):** Every plan carries an explicit STRIDE threat register (`T-06.49-01` through `T-06.49-14` and `T-06.49-SC`) mapped in `.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-VALIDATION.md:52-66`.

### Explicit Disposition of Prior Finding NEW-2
- **NEW-2 (LOW — ambient-recorder CI pass `CV_ACCESS_RECORD_REQUESTS=1` scope): CLOSED (Planning Closure; Execution Reporting Pending Wave 5).**
  - **Source & Plan Evidence:** `noxfile.py:49-57` runs a second full-suite pass with `CV_ACCESS_RECORD_REQUESTS="1"`, which activates the autouse fixture `record_access_requests` in `tests/conftest.py:9-21` (`with recording(recorder):`). In Cycle 4 (`0ca3d58b`), two acceptable closures were offered: either add a focused `CV_ACCESS_RECORD_REQUESTS=1` check or explicitly document that local run as omitted and uncertified. Revision `b3c635b0b6f7c394f96619b8d07eeb786ca12321` adopts the second permitted option across:
    1. `.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-05-PLAN.md:30` (`must_haves.truths`),
    2. `.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-05-PLAN.md:127` (Task `05-03` `<action>`),
    3. `.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-05-PLAN.md:133` (Task `05-03` `<acceptance_criteria>`),
    4. `.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-05-PLAN.md:238-249` (Round 4 Review Dispositions Ledger anchored to `0ca3d58b`),
    5. `.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-05-PLAN.md:257-258` (`<output>`), and
    6. `.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-VALIDATION.md:29-35, 121` (Test Infrastructure limitation note and Validation Sign-Off checklist).
  - **Technical Justification Accepted:** In `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:151-170, 208-211` and `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/ancestors.py:88-105`, `_get_guardian_checker(user)` only replaces `ObjectPermissionChecker(user)` acquisition prior to `has_perm` evaluation and does not interact with `AccessRecorder`. Keeping `CV_ACCESS_RECORD_REQUESTS=0` in local verification commands prevents ambient recording overhead from perturbing SQL query measurements, while forbidding any claim that trace-parity tests certify ambient-recording mode and mandating explicit disclosure of the unexecuted `CV_ACCESS_RECORD_REQUESTS=1` pass in `06.49-VALIDATION.md`, `06.49-05-SUMMARY.md`, and final reporting.

## Consensus Summary — Cycle 5

### Agreed strengths and prior closures

All three source-cited reviewers explicitly accept the selected NEW-2 disposition and confirm that all earlier closures remain intact. They support the bounded user-owned checker, grant reuse without cached decisions, preserved actor/database/trace boundaries, measured before/after costs, retained-user lifetime documentation, normal next-request freshness and receipt-backed publication. These are signed-off plan obligations; all implementation and execution evidence remains pending.

| Finding | Current disposition and evidence |
| --- | --- |
| NEW-2 LOW | **CLOSED / explicitly dispositioned — all three.** Plan 05:30,127,133,238–248,257 requires the omitted `CV_ACCESS_RECORD_REQUESTS=1` CI pass to be reported as unexecuted and uncertified in VALIDATION, final SUMMARY and final reporting. Trace parity may not certify ambient recording. VALIDATION:29–35,121 mirrors it. `noxfile.py:47–57` and `tests/conftest.py:8–21` independently confirm the mode and fixture behavior. Fable, which raised the item, expressly accepts this option as one of its two offered closures. |

| H-02 HIGH | Remains **CLOSED — all three**. Plan 02:109,113 creates the local anonymous backing user and patches Guardian's imported setting before lookup/construction. `tests/test1/conftest.py:128` disables the backing user by default; the working patch precedent is `tests/test1/test_delete_databases.py:32–42`. |
| A-01 MEDIUM | Incorporated and signed off by all three. Plan 05:126,140–143 includes all four affected cost modules in both cached Django 4.2/5.2 six-worker selections. |
| A-02 MEDIUM | Incorporated and signed off by all three. Plan 05:103 assigns duplicated card-list, nested and existing migration cost/lifetime prose; `docs/reference/card-list-view.md:326`, `docs/reference/nested.md:584`, `docs/migrating-1.0.md:156` remain the existing targets. |

Plan shorthand in this synthesis resolves to `06.49-0N-PLAN.md` within `.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385`; the complete current quote inventory is in Verification coverage and source-grounding.json.

### Agreed concerns

None. All three reviewers request no further PLAN amendment. No HIGH was raised or suppressed. The remaining unexecuted recorder mode is an accepted verification limit, not a passed test or a demonstrated runtime defect.

### Divergent views and non-actionable notes

- Fable considers “isolate permission-query measurements from ambient recorder activity” a weak rationale because the recorder itself issues no SQL, but explicitly says the wording can stay and the disposition does not depend on it. The coordinator confirms `AccessRecorder.record` builds records and `capture_filter` retains unevaluated references (`src/crud_views/lib/access_trace.py:67–90`); neither the synthesis nor the plan claims a measured SQL increase caused by ambient recording. Gemini's stronger suggestion that overhead would perturb SQL measurements is reviewer interpretation, not established measurement evidence. No amendment is required to the honest omission/reporting contract.
- Fable could not check executable permissions inside its review environment. A separate read-only coordinator check confirms the seven referenced interpreter/Ruff/MkDocs paths exist and have execute permission (`executable-path-check.json`). This establishes availability only, not a test or build pass.
- Intermediate failures, unknown after-query numbers, supported-series results and final evidence remain explicit execution obligations. Existing dispositions for the unused delegate parameter, RequestFactory alternative, duplicate baseline constants, forecasts and receipt directories stand without a demonstrated contradiction.

## Claude Review (fable-review) — Cycle 4

# Cross-AI Plan Review: Phase 06.49, Convergence Cycle 4

**Verdict:** converged. NEW-1 is closed at planning level, no prior closure regressed, and I found no HIGH or MEDIUM defect. One new LOW item (NEW-2, recorder-mode CI pass) is cheap to close but not blocking.

**Access and method:** I had read access to the repository at `c9db9093` and read the source cited below. I ran no tests or measurements and edited nothing. Every statement about plan execution is planning closure, not execution evidence.

## 06.49-01

**Summary.** Captures the D-38 list-page baseline and the navbar, ancestor and child receipts on unchanged production. Since `c9db9093` it also runs the four-module combined collection before any production change.

**Strengths**
- The fixture exists as described: `GuardianPublisherListView` has `cv_list_actions = ["detail", "update", "delete"]` on the module-global `cv_guardian_publisher` (`tests/test1/app/views.py:709-719`).
- Each row action is a separate Guardian `cv_has_access(user, row)` call (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:939-945`), reaching the constructor at `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:208-211`. The baseline therefore measures the per-action construction that D-38 is about.
- Asserting actions inside the action region is needed. `PublisherTable.id` is a `LinkDetailColumn` that builds a detail URL with no access check (`tests/test1/app/views.py:282-284`, `src/crud_views/lib/table/columns.py:47-54`), so a detail href exists outside the action cell.
- The expected pass counts match the source:
  - Navbar: 3×2×5×2×2 = 120 cells (`tests/test1/test_navbar_costs.py:90-95`).
  - Child selection: 2+2+1 = 5 cases (`tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1133-1135, 1290-1292, 1319-1320`).
  - Ancestor: 4+4+1 = 9 JSON slices (`tests/test1/test_ancestor_costs.py:204-208, 263-266, 317`).
- The combined before command in 01-02 uses the same four modules and flags as 04-02's combined gate; only the JUnit path differs.

**Concerns**
- **LOW, not actionable.** The reset-then-warm protocol is introduced in 01-02, while 01-01 only says "warm … explicitly". If 01-01 pins without the reset, 01-02 must re-pin and rerun the isolated command, which is not in 01-02's verify block. The 01-02 action ("remeasure and rerun both commands") and its acceptance criterion ("pass both serially and in the combined…") already require it. No amendment needed.
- **Execution note, not a finding.** `cv_guardian_publisher` is shared by every test in an xdist worker. The reset must restore `_default_permissions` and `_VOCAB_CACHE` in a `finally`, as the named pattern does (`tests/test1/test_navbar_costs.py:62-68`). "Using the existing navbar cache_conditions pattern" covers this.

**Suggestions.** Optionally implement reset-then-warm in 01-01 so the first pin is the final one.

**Risk:** LOW.

## 06.49-02

**Summary.** Adds `_get_guardian_checker(user)`, replaces the four constructions, keeps `_request_checker` as a stateless delegate, and adds the decision-independence and anonymous regressions.

**Strengths**
- The four sites are exactly where the plan says: `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:153, 210` and `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/ancestors.py:67, 70`. A repository-wide grep of `src`, `docs`, `tests` and `examples` finds no fifth construction in package code.
- A local import in the helper keeps existing mocks valid:
  - `tests/test1/test_access_trace_guardian.py:53-57` uses a `SimpleNamespace` user with a patched `guardian.core.ObjectPermissionChecker`; plain `getattr`/`setattr` works on it and `factory.call_count` stays 1.
  - `tests/test1/test_access_trace_guardian.py:514-528` still gets three `has_perm` calls on one stored mock checker.
  - `tests/test1/test_access_trace_guardian.py:247` patches `ancestors._request_checker`, which the retained delegate preserves.
- The H-02 mechanism is correct:
  - `ANONYMOUS_USER_NAME` is copied into `guardian.conf.settings` at import (`guardian/conf/settings.py:7-15` in the cached environment).
  - `get_anonymous_user()` reads it at call time (`guardian/utils.py:66-88`).
  - The suite sets it to `None` (`tests/test1/conftest.py:128`).
  - The patch pattern the plan cites exists (`tests/test1/test_delete_databases.py:32-42`).
- F-01 targets the right assertions: the two actor comprehensions at `tests/test1/test_link_access.py:469, 476`. The dynamic gate/own/state loop at `:478-496` stays untouched.

**Concerns.** None new. The unused `view` parameter and the intermediate-failure inventory are recorded dispositions, and I found nothing that contradicts them.

**Suggestions.** None.

**Risk:** LOW.

## 06.49-03

**Summary.** Pins D-37 (retained-user grants, next-request freshness, permission-only workflow change) and adapts F-05 and F-06.

**Strengths**
- The workflow mechanics match the plan:

- F-06 is feasible as written. The test makes two real `client.get` calls (`tests/test1/test_ancestor_boundaries.py:541-545`). The existing line `:550` shows a Guardian checker is built in all four cases, so `request.user._cv_guardian_checker` will exist to compare.
- F-05 cancel needs the reassignment the plan specifies. The view is bound once with `user` (`tests/test1/test_cancel_permissions.py:632`) and the grant follows at `:635`. A refetch that is not assigned to `view.request.user` would not reach the consumer.
- F-05 parity matches `tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:611-615`.

**Concerns.** None new.

**Suggestions.** None.

**Risk:** LOW.

## 06.49-04

**Summary.** Re-measures list, navbar, child and ancestor costs after the helper lands, with the H-01 setup correction and the N-01 receipt.

**Strengths**
- H-01 is correctly diagnosed. The mixed loop loads the parent's change-only grants (`tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1335-1339`), then `view` is granted at `:1353`, then the same actor is checked at `:1358-1360`. Moving the grant before the loop fixes this without a refetch.
- The plan's "exact first/subsequent" acceptance covers the lower-bound assertions that will also break on a cache hit: `:1153` (warm pass) and `:1341` (second renderer), in addition to the equalities at `:1306-1307` and `:1361-1362`.
- N-01's premise holds. `test_query_plain_child_link_cost` refetches a user per layer (`:1098`), so both layers are first-load observations.
- The navbar reconciliation targets the right formula (`tests/test1/test_navbar_costs.py:163-165`). `cache_conditions` only deletes Django's three permission caches (`:39-43`), so the retained checker survives as the plan requires.

**Concerns.** None new.

**Suggestions.** None.

**Risk:** LOW to MEDIUM. This is execution risk only: the after numbers are unknown until measured, which the plan treats correctly.

## Cross-plan analysis

- **Ordering.** The before measurement precedes production change. 01-02's combined gate is now the last step before plan 02, and 02-01 carries an explicit precondition.
- **Probe reconciliation.** The concept's 101 probe failures are fully allocated: F-01 (1), F-02 (2), F-03 (5), F-04 (80), F-05 (8+1) and F-06 (4) sum to 101.
- **Scope.** No plan touches `link_access.py`, workflow production code, list filtering, delete or bulk. No prefetch, decision cache or freshness bypass appears.
- **Security.** Acquisition stays after the actor, database and fallback branches at all four sites. The key threats (user and request isolation, target independence, workflow scope and state) each map to a named test.

### Divergent view — current actionable finding

- **NEW-2 — LOW (Fable): record the ambient-recorder verification scope in task 05-03.** The second nox suite pass uses `CV_ACCESS_RECORD_REQUESTS=1` (`noxfile.py:47–57`); the root autouse fixture then creates and enters a recorder in every test (`tests/conftest.py:8–21`). Those branches sit beside the changed Guardian acquisition paths (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:153–170`, `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/ancestors.py:88–104`). All 24 planned pytest commands explicitly set `CV_ACCESS_RECORD_REQUESTS=0`, including the final and supported-series gates (`06.49-05-PLAN.md:135–143`). Task 05-03 limits installed-distribution and full Python-matrix claims (`:126`) but does not explicitly name this recorder pass as unrun or disposition it.

  **Needed plan decision:** add the focused recorder-enabled checker/cost/workflow command supplied in Fable's report to task 05-03, with a failure condition and actual result receipt; **or** explicitly defer/reject that local run and require the unexecuted `CV_ACCESS_RECORD_REQUESTS=1` CI pass to be listed as a verification limitation. The latter is the reviewer's stated minimum and preserves the bounded scope without another broad suite. Existing direct trace tests remain useful but do not explicitly cover ambient recording around the new list/constructor-count tests.

  This is a verification-scope/documentation gap, **not a demonstrated runtime defect**, a measured query change, or a request to redesign tracing. `AccessRecorder.capture_filter` retains references without evaluating queries (`src/crud_views/lib/access_trace.py:87–90`), and Fable expects the pass to succeed. The coordinator independently confirmed the source premise and all 24 command environments; `new2-evidence.json` records those lines and hashes. No recorder-mode test was executed during planning.

Fable labels the overall verdict “converged” and NEW-2 “non-blocking,” but also asks for a concrete task amendment or explicit limitation. The convergence contract counts actionable non-HIGH findings until executable PLAN coverage or an explicit PLAN disposition exists. Neither exists for NEW-2, so it counts once. No reviewer substitution, silent acceptance or unauthorized plan edit is used to erase it.

## Claude Review (fable-review) — Cycle 3

# Cross-AI Plan Review: Phase 06.49, Cycle 3

**Verdict: converged.** 0 HIGH, 0 MEDIUM, and one new LOW (a one-command addition to plan 01-01, not a blocker). N-01 to N-04 are closed at planning level, and none of the six cycle-1 closures regressed.

I read the repository at `a550c54b`. Source, tests and docs are unchanged since cycle 2, and the concept is unchanged against `4b65990f`. I ran no tests or measurements and edited nothing. All closures below are planning closures; execution evidence is still pending for every plan.

## 06.49-01

**Summary.** Measurement-first plan: a real Guardian list response with row actions for 0/1/3 rows, plus before receipts for navbar, ancestor and the five child cases, all before any production edit.

**Strengths**
- The fixture exists as described: `GuardianPublisherListView` with `cv_list_actions = ["detail", "update", "delete"]` on `cv_guardian_publisher` (`tests/test1/app/views.py:709-719`).
- The four constructors and the view tuple the plan must attest as unchanged are where it says: `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:153, 210` and `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/ancestors.py:67, 70-71`.
- The counts match source: 3×2×5×2×2 = 120 navbar cells (`tests/test1/test_navbar_costs.py:91-95`) and nine ancestor JSON labels (`tests/test1/test_ancestor_costs.py:204-208, 317`).
- The round-1 disposition about asserting inside the row-action region is justified: `PublisherTable` has `id = LinkDetailColumn()` (`tests/test1/app/views.py:282-284`), so a detail link exists outside the action cell.

**Concerns**
- HIGH: none.
- MEDIUM: none.
- LOW (new, NEW-1): the exact before pin is only ever run serially, alone, in a fresh process. Its first run inside the combined `-n 6` collection is plan 04-02, after production has changed.
  - If the pin turns out to depend on collection order or cache state there, the before figure under a corrected protocol can no longer be measured, and 01-01 itself forbids reconstructing it.
  - RESEARCH already records this kind of difference (six versus seven queries from ContentType warm-up in the 06.48 baseline).
  - 04-02's "keep both observations" cannot apply to a single exact-integer pin that must pass in the combined command.
  - Counter-argument: a correctly written reset-then-warm protocol, as in `cache_conditions` (`tests/test1/test_navbar_costs.py:34-68`), is order-independent, so this is a risk and not a certain defect.

**Suggestion.** Add one pre-change verify to 01-01 (exact text under "New findings").

**Risk:** LOW.

## 06.49-02

**Summary.** Adds `_get_guardian_checker(user)`, replaces the four construction sites, keeps `_request_checker` as a thin delegate, and adds the anonymous and decision-independence tests plus the F-01 exemption.

**Strengths**
- The delegate decision is sound: the only private-seam patch is `monkeypatch.setattr(ancestors, "_request_checker", factory)` (`tests/test1/test_access_trace_guardian.py:247`), so that test needs no rewrite.
- The local import inside the helper keeps both constructor monkeypatches working (`tests/test1/test_access_trace_guardian.py:57, 514`).
  - The first uses a `SimpleNamespace` user, which accepts `setattr`.
  - The three-answer test still sees three `has_perm` calls on the one stored mock (`:521-529`).
- F-01 is located exactly: the two user-attribute comprehensions are at `tests/test1/test_link_access.py:469, 476`, and the dynamic gate/own/state loop the plan keeps follows directly.
- H-02 is correctly grounded:
  - The suite sets `ANONYMOUS_USER_NAME=None` (`tests/test1/conftest.py:128`).
  - Guardian reads `guardian_settings` at call time (installed `guardian/utils.py:86`).
  - The precedent patch is `tests/test1/test_delete_databases.py:42`.
- Guardian's `has_perm` reads `is_active` and `is_superuser` live on each call, before the grant cache (installed `guardian/core.py:76-82`). A shared checker therefore cannot freeze those actor flags.

**Concerns**
- HIGH: none.
- MEDIUM: none.
- LOW: none actionable.
  - Observation only: Guardian's cache key is `(ctype.id, pk)` with no database alias (installed `guardian/core.py:189-192`). Nested paths are pinned to `default` by `_check_nested_auth` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:176, 199`), and I found no stock path that checks the same model and PK on two aliases in one user lifetime. The 05-03 full suite covers the multi-database tests. No amendment needed.

**Risk:** LOW.

## 06.49-03

**Summary.** Lifetime evidence: retained-user staleness, next-request freshness, the permission-only workflow change, and the F-05/F-06 fixture adaptations.

**Strengths**
- F-02 mechanics match source. The reload is `self.get_object(queryset=...)` after the refusal (`src/crud_views_workflow/lib/views.py:96-103`), and the test revokes before `super().cv_form_valid_process` (`tests/test1/test_workflow_review.py:230-235`), so before the atomic block. The plan's "remains revoked, state rolled back" is accurate.
- The plan's instruction to run the original assertions before the follow-up request is necessary. `checked` and `loads` are appended by the same class the follow-up hits (`tests/test1/test_workflow_review.py:217-224`), and the assertions at `:265-270` would otherwise see a third entry.
- F-05 sites are exact:
  - Parity: grant at `tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:611`, reuse at `:615`.
  - Cancel: grant at `tests/test1/test_cancel_permissions.py:635`. Its `bound()` holds the user only on `request.user` (`:36-43`), so assigning the re-fetched actor there is sufficient.
- F-06 lines are exact: `tests/test1/test_ancestor_boundaries.py:550, 554`. The stale docstring that A-04 targets is at `:13-17`.

**Concerns**
- HIGH: none.
- MEDIUM: none.
- LOW: none new. The temporary URLconf for the local workflow class is an executor detail, and the rejected RequestFactory alternative has a stated rationale I see no contradiction in.

**Risk:** LOW.

## 06.49-04

**Summary.** Measured after pins for list, navbar, child and ancestor surfaces, plus the retained child-link receipt (N-01) and the H-01 fixture fix.

**Strengths**
- N-01's receipt mechanism is real, not a new harness:
  - `test_query_plain_child_link_cost` already emits `record_property("measurement", ...)` through `report_measurement` (`tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1069-1072, 1122-1130`).
  - The module is in the existing combined command.
  - `junit_family=legacy` avoids the xunit2 property warning.
- The plan's "both layers are first-load" statement is correct: each layer re-fetches the user (`:1098`). The matrix is 16 cells (`:234, 1076-1078`).
- H-01 matches source: `world` grants only `change` (`:649`), the mixed loop loads those grants (`:1335-1338`), and `view` is granted afterwards (`:1353`). Moving the grant ahead of the loop is the smallest fix.
- The two stale comments are named correctly (`:1238, 1352`).
- "Keep the checker when measuring navbar work" matches the harness: `cache_conditions` deletes only Django's three model-permission caches (`tests/test1/test_navbar_costs.py:39-43`). The comment to rewrite is at `:44-45`.
- "Ancestor pins may stay byte-identical" is plausible: that module pins gate events and, for plain providers only, `rest == legacy_total` (`tests/test1/test_ancestor_costs.py:250-252`). The Guardian helper gate is counted by spy, not SQL (`:262`).

**Concerns**
- HIGH: none.
- MEDIUM: none.
- LOW: none actionable.
  - Note for the executor, already within the plan's "separate ContentType from grants" rule: a cache hit still calls `get_content_type(obj)` (installed `guardian/core.py:168-170`), so cold-vocabulary navbar cells keep ContentType queries even when grant SELECTs drop to zero.
  - After H-01 the ordinary-control equality (`:1361`) likely becomes trivial. The plan already discloses this as a setup change and not an optimization comparison.

**Risk:** LOW.

## Cross-plan analysis

- **Ordering** is sound: before receipts (01), then helper (02), lifetime (03), after pins (04), docs and gate (05). No same-wave file overlap.

### Agreed concerns

None newly raised by two reviewers. No reviewer raised a HIGH. Grok and Gemini require no amendment; their clean verdicts do not suppress a single reviewer's actionable non-HIGH finding.

### Divergent view — current actionable finding

- **NEW-1 — LOW (Fable): verify the new list baseline under combined collection before production changes, or explicitly disposition that risk.** Plan 01 currently verifies the three list cases only in an isolated serial process (`06.49-01-PLAN.md:76,79`); the first explicit combined four-cost-module command is after implementation (`06.49-04-PLAN.md:113`). Plan 01 already requires explicit warm-up and measured exact pins (`:68`), and `tests/test1/test_navbar_costs.py:34–68` provides a controlled cache pattern, so this is a verification precaution, **not a demonstrated defect or assertion that the future test will be flaky**. The existing phase-06.48 receipt at `06.48-COST-BASELINE.md:570–587` documents a real isolated/combined ContentType difference in another test. If the new protocol needs correction only after implementation, comparable before evidence would require additional work on the original source; it must never be reconstructed from changed production.

  **Needed plan decision:** in plan 01, add the existing combined four-cost-module six-worker command before plan 02, with acceptance that all three list pins match the isolated run and any protocol correction is remeasured on unchanged production; alternatively explicitly defer/reject this extra verification with the maintainability and controlled-protocol rationale. No new harness is requested. The current plan has neither this verification nor a disposition specific to it. The Round 1 deferral of duplicate constants and the plan-04 deferral of expanded historical child receipts do not explicitly decide this new list-protocol suggestion.

Fable labels its overall verdict “converged” and says this LOW is not a blocker, but also supplies a required plan amendment. Under the convergence contract, its actionability is determined by executable PLAN coverage or explicit PLAN disposition. Neither exists yet for NEW-1, so it counts once; no unauthorized plan edit or acceptance has been inferred.

## Claude Review (fable-review) — Cycle 2

# Cross-AI Plan Review — Phase 06.49, Cycle 2 (plans at `3b7dfe9f`)

**Verdict:** both prior HIGH findings and all four actionable non-HIGH findings are closed at planning level. I found no new HIGH. One new MEDIUM (a published cost table has no planned receipt) and three new LOW items remain.

**Basis:** read-only inspection of the checkout and of installed django-guardian 3.5.2 / Django 6.0 under `.nox/tests-3-12-django-6-0`. I ran no tests or measurements and edited nothing. Every statement below is traced by reading; closures still depend on execution evidence.

Two facts apply to all plans:

- **The probe inventory still describes this tree.** `git log 63e8e07d..HEAD -- src tests docs examples CHANGELOG.md` is empty; only planning and concept files changed since the probe ran.
- **No further masked failure found.** I re-read each failing test family from concept 12.7 past its first failing assertion. H-01 was the only second failure.

---

## 06.49-01

### Summary
Captures before-figures on unchanged production code. Unchanged in substance since cycle 1; the ledger now states the classifier and action-cell requirements.

### Strengths
- **The fixture hits the changed path.** `GuardianPublisherListView` declares three row actions (`tests/test1/app/views.py:716-719`); each object check builds its own checker today (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:208-211`).
- **Counts match source.** Navbar is 3×2×5×2×2 = 120 cells (`tests/test1/test_navbar_costs.py:91-95`); the `-k` selection yields five child cases (`tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1133-1134, 1290-1291, 1319-1320`).
- **Construction sites are still exactly four** (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:153, 210`; `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/ancestors.py:67, 70`).

### Concerns
- **HIGH:** none.
- **MEDIUM:** none.
- **LOW — Fixed receipt directory can keep stale files.** `_write` uses `os.makedirs(out, exist_ok=True)` (`tests/test1/test_ancestor_costs.py:181-186`), so a retried run into `/tmp/cv0649-ancestor-before` keeps JSON from an aborted attempt. The plan says "fresh" but hard-codes the path.

### Suggestions
- Remove or uniquely name the ancestor receipt directory before each capture.

### Risk
**LOW.**

---

## 06.49-02

### Summary
Adds the user-owned checker helper, swaps the four sites, and adds isolation, decision-independence and anonymous tests. The anonymous test is now executable as described.

### Strengths
- **The H-02 fix matches Guardian's code** (verdict and evidence in the closure table below).
- **Any Guardian view works for the direct anonymous call.** `_check_nested_auth` returns at once for top-level views and skips the actor check for a non-model actor (`src/crud_views/lib/ancestor_db.py:155-158`).
- **No test passes a `Mock` user into a Guardian path.** A `getattr(user, name, None)` helper would return a truthy auto-attribute for one; the trace tests use `SimpleNamespace` (`tests/test1/test_access_trace_guardian.py:54, 244`).
- **The retained delegate keeps the only private seam** (`tests/test1/test_access_trace_guardian.py:247`); no other reference to `_request_checker` exists in `src/` or `tests/`.

### Concerns
- **HIGH:** none.
- **MEDIUM:** none.
- **LOW:** none new. Keeping the unused `view` parameter is acceptable as long as the docstring says it is unused.

### Suggestions
- None required.

### Risk
**LOW.**

---

## 06.49-03

### Summary
Proves the D-37 lifetime and adapts F-02, F-05 and F-06. The boundary-module docstring is now in scope.

### Strengths
- **The cancel adaptation is necessary and sufficient.** The first `cv_get_cancel_url()` loads an empty Book grant set, so the grant at `tests/test1/test_cancel_permissions.py:635` is invisible to the same user; a fresh actor on the bound request restores `events == [book]` at `:637`.
- **The workflow revoke survives the refusal.** `remove_perm` runs before `super().cv_form_valid_process` opens the atomic block (`tests/test1/test_workflow_review.py:230-235`; `src/crud_views_workflow/lib/views.py:128-134`), so a next-request 403 is real.
- **F-06 is the only consumer of the retired attribute** (`tests/test1/test_ancestor_boundaries.py:550, 554`).

### Concerns
- **HIGH:** none.
- **MEDIUM:** none.
- **LOW (carried, disposition accepted):** the Client-plus-temporary-URLconf follow-up is the most intricate mechanic in the phase. It is feasible and test-only, and the plan orders the original assertions first.

### Suggestions
- None required.

### Risk
**LOW.**

---

## 06.49-04

### Summary
Reconciles pins from after-measurements. The H-01 fix is correct. One evidence gap remains for the documentation that plan 05 builds on these receipts.

### Strengths
- **Moving the grant resolves H-01 without any refresh behaviour** (verdict and evidence in the closure table below).
- **Only one pin in that test needs changing afterwards:** `permissions > 0` on the second renderer (`tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1341`), which the plan's "exact first/subsequent" rule covers.
- **The mixed-loop figures stay comparable despite the setup change.** A checker load costs the same queries whatever number of grants it finds (`guardian/core.py:178-183`).

### Concerns
- **HIGH:** none.
- **MEDIUM — N-01: the published child-link table has no planned receipt.**
  - The table in `docs/reference/context_buttons.md:228-238` uses the axes of `test_query_plain_child_link_cost` (`tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1075-1130`).
  - No plan names that test. Receipts are captured only for the list, the navbar and the five F-03 cases (`.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-04-PLAN.md:81, 83, 94`).
  - Plan 05 must rewrite that table with "first-load versus subsequent-render" values and trace every figure to plan-04 receipts (`.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-05-PLAN.md:80, 86`). As written, that criterion cannot be met for this table.
  - The test re-fetches its user per layer (`:1098`), so its 8/6/4/2 figures are first-load values and probably unchanged. That is reasoning, not a measurement, and the table is labelled Django 5.2 while plan 04 measures on Django 6.0.
  - The round-1 deferral (`.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-04-PLAN.md:152`) covers *before* receipts for all child cases; it does not cover a current receipt for the published table.
- **LOW:** none.

### Suggestions
- Add to 04-02: run `test_query_plain_child_link_cost` on the final tree with `--junitxml` and record its sixteen-cell-per-provider measurements in `06.49-COST-BASELINE.md` as "first load, fresh user".

### Risk
**LOW–MEDIUM** until N-01 is written in; **LOW** afterwards.

---

## 06.49-05

### Summary
Publishes lifetime and cost documentation, adds the release note and closes regression evidence. All three cycle-1 gaps are now planned.

### Strengths
- **The stale-prose inventory is complete.** A grep of `docs/`, `README.md`, `CHANGELOG.md` and `src/` finds checker-lifetime prose only in the six pages the plan lists plus the Guardian ancestor module docstring that plan 02 owns.
- **No documentation test pins the sentences being rewritten.** In `tests/test1` the phrases appear only in two code comments (`test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1238`, `test_ancestor_costs.py:23`).
- **The example suite needs no adaptation.** No example test calls an access hook directly; grant changes are followed by Client requests or a re-fetched user (`examples/bootstrap5/navbar_demo/tests.py:170-183`, `examples/bootstrap5/guardian_demo/tests.py:90-99`).
- **Environments and targets exist.** All three cached Django series are present, each with django-guardian 3.5.2, and the unreleased `### Changed` heading is in place (`CHANGELOG.md:3, 450`).

### Concerns
- **HIGH:** none.
- **MEDIUM:** none here; N-01 lands in this plan's documentation but belongs to plan 04.
- **LOW — N-02: two verify commands contain `&amp;&amp;`.**
  - `.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-05-PLAN.md:142, 144`.
  - Phase 06.48 plans use a literal `&&` in `<automated>` blocks.
  - Run literally, the Ruff line backgrounds `ruff check` and returns only the format check's status, so a lint failure would be hidden.
- **LOW — N-03: version reconciliation edits files this plan does not own.** 05-03 sends a Django 4.2/5.2 pin difference back to "the affected plan-04 task" (`...06.49-05-PLAN.md:125`), but the four cost modules are absent from this plan's `files_modified` and the docs from 05-01/05-02 would already be written.
- **LOW — N-04: the retained-user caveat omits cached or pickled users.**
  - 05-01 names tests, shell and background work.
  - Django pickles a model's whole `__dict__` (`django/db/models/base.py:637-639`), so a project that caches user objects between requests would carry loaded grants into the request gate. That is my inference from the code, not something I tested.
  - Django's own permission cache behaves the same, which is the comparison D-05 already draws.

### Suggestions
- Replace `&amp;&amp;` with `&&` on both lines.
- Either add the four cost modules on Django 4.2 and 5.2 to 04-02's verification, or add them to this plan's `files_modified` with a rule to re-check published figures after any pin change.
- Add "a user object cached or pickled between requests" to the lifetime paragraph in `docs/reference/guardian.md`.

### Risk
**LOW.**

## 06.49-01

**Summary:** The before-measurement wave is correctly ordered and uses a real list response. It does not change production checkers.

**Strengths:** `GuardianPublisherListView` has detail/update/delete row actions (`tests/test1/app/views.py:716-719`). Those render through `cv_get_list_action_group` into `cv-col-action` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:849-851`, `src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/columns/actions.html:2-8`, `src/crud_views/lib/table/attrs.py:86`). Each target calls `cv_has_access`, which currently builds a fresh checker (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:944-945`, `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:208-211`). List filtering stays on `get_objects_for_user` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:338-346`). The four constructors are still only `mixins.py:153`, `mixins.py:210`, `ancestors.py:67`, and `ancestors.py:70`. The five child cases are exactly the `-k` selection (`test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1133-1158`, `1290-1316`, `1319-1371`).

**HIGH:** none.

**MEDIUM:** none. The old classifier warning is already an executable constraint: checker grant SELECTs must be distinct from list-filter subqueries, and relabeling `sql_categories`'s broad `permissions` bucket (`test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1045-1046`) does not satisfy it.

**LOW:** none requiring amendment. "Each Details link" at zero rows is vacuous. Resolved URLs in the action cell avoid `PublisherTable.id = LinkDetailColumn()` (`tests/test1/app/views.py:282-284`).

**Suggestions:** none.

**Risk:** LOW. Pins are not yet measured.

## 06.49-02

**Summary:** One user-owned checker replaces the four constructors without sharing decisions. The anonymous regression is now configured the way Guardian actually looks up its backing user.

**Strengths:** Acquisition stays after inactive, model-fallback, and no-object branches (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:141-212`, `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/ancestors.py:81-88`). `get_objects_for_user` is untouched. The trace seam patches `_request_checker` itself (`tests/test1/test_access_trace_guardian.py:247-258`). The three-answer test counts `has_perm`, not constructions (`tests/test1/test_access_trace_guardian.py:512-528`). F-01's two attribute checks are at `tests/test1/test_link_access.py:469` and `:476`. A local `from guardian.core import ObjectPermissionChecker` still sees the constructor monkeypatch.

**HIGH:** none.

**MEDIUM:** none.

**LOW:** none. Keeping the unused `view` argument is a maintainable private seam; Ruff's selected rules do not flag it (`pyproject.toml:133-134`).

**Suggestions:** none.

**Risk:** LOW.

## 06.49-03

**Summary:** D-37 is pinned as same-user staleness plus a fresh next request, without a refresh bypass. F-05 and F-06 are narrow fixture repairs.

**Strengths:** Workflow refusal reloads through scoped `get_object` after unwind (`src/crud_views_workflow/lib/views.py:96-103`). Only `outcome=permission` removes the grant before that reload (`tests/test1/test_workflow_review.py:230-235`, `259-270`). `user_campaign_change` has model change, not an object grant (`tests/test1/conftest.py:759-764`), and the mixin default rejects model fallback (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:99`). The local classes inherit `cv_viewset`, so they are not re-registered. Cancel's second check needs a new user because the first check caches the book's empty grants (`tests/test1/test_cancel_permissions.py:633-637`). Parity's `client.get` is already a new request user; `rendered_data` is the same Python user (`tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:611-615`). F-06 is four real Client cases (`tests/test1/test_ancestor_boundaries.py:49-54`, `538-554`). `SimpleLazyObject.__setattr__` forwards to `_wrapped` (`django/utils/functional.py:289-296`).

**HIGH:** none.

**MEDIUM:** none.

**LOW:** none. A RequestFactory follow-up would not prove middleware user lifetime. The temporary URLconf is justified because `/campaign/.../workflow/` is not the local class.

**Suggestions:** none.

**Risk:** LOW.

## 06.49-04

**Summary:** After-counts stay measurement-driven. The ordinary-control stale grant is removed by preparing both grants before the first load, not by refreshing the checker.

**Strengths:** `world` grants only change (`tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:648-649`). The mixed loop reaches the ancestor checker (`:1335-1341`, `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/ancestors.py:88`). Guardian then caches every codename for that object (`guardian/core.py:170-186`), so a later `view` grant is invisible to `cv_has_access` at `:1358`. Moving that existing `user_guardian_object_perm(..., "view", ...)` before the loop makes the first load include it. `CHANGE_READ` still requires change (`test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:42-45`). The same actor, allow result, and no-parent-gate assertion remain. The plan forbids `refresh_from_db`, attribute deletion, and a production freshness exception, and it keeps the plan-01 receipt as the original setup.

Navbar isolation clears only Django perm caches (`tests/test1/test_navbar_costs.py:39-47`) and asserts hooks separately from the count formula (`:149-165`). Ancestor warm requests are new Client users, so `auth_permission > 0` can remain (`tests/test1/test_ancestor_costs.py:197-202`).

**HIGH:** none.

**MEDIUM:** none.

**LOW:** `permissions > 0` is asserted on both discovery temperatures and both mixed renderers (`test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1153`, `:1341`). After sharing, the second observation can be zero. That is a count pin, not a parent-gate semantic. The task already requires measured first/subsequent pins and forbids a refetch, so this does not reopen H-01.

**Suggestions:** none required. If edited, say those two lower bounds follow the measured second observation.

**Risk:** LOW. Numerical savings are not established until execution.

## 06.49-05

**Summary:** Documentation and regression closure match the approved lifetime and the measured-cost boundary. No Breaking entry is introduced.

**Strengths:** Stale sentences are still at `docs/reference/navbar.md:346-347`, `docs/reference/context_buttons.md:244-246`, `docs/reference/guardian.md:465-468`, `docs/reference/card-list-view.md:321-326`, `docs/reference/nested.md:580-584`, and `docs/migrating-1.0.md:156`. The custom `ObjectPermissionChecker` recipe at `docs/reference/guardian.md:242-244` stays outside the package helper, which matches the plan's package-path limit. Cached interpreters exist for Django 4.2, 5.2, and 6.0; `.venv/bin/mkdocs` and `.venv/bin/ruff` exist. The 4.2/5.2 commands include all four cost modules with `-n 6`.

## Cross-Plan Analysis & Cycle 2 Prior-Finding Closure Review

### Wave Structure, Scope, Security, and Goal Achievement
- **Wave structure (5 sequential waves, 11 tasks: 2+2+2+2+3):** File ownership across waves is conflict-free, and every task has executable `<verify>` commands with explicit `<fails_when>` conditions.
- **Scope and maintainability (`CLAUDE.md`, D-01–D-09):** Production edits are strictly limited to the new private helper in `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/permissions.py` and the four checker acquisition sites in `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:153, 210` and `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/ancestors.py:61-72` (plus its module docstring at `lines 21-23`). No decision caching, cross-request caching, `prefetch_perms`, public helper API, or stage-3 link-check changes are introduced.
- **Security (ASVS 4.0.3 L1):** Checker ownership is strictly bound to the Python user instance (`user._cv_guardian_checker`), positioned after inactive-actor, default-database (`_check_nested_auth`), permission-vocabulary, and model-fallback checks. Normal requests receive distinct user instances and distinct checkers; D-37 same-request grant reuse and retained-user lifetime are covered by both automated regressions and documentation.

### Agreed Concerns

None newly raised by two reviewers. Grok and Gemini require no plan amendment. Their clean verdicts do not suppress Fable's independently grounded non-HIGH findings.

### Divergent Views — current actionable findings

- **N-01 — MEDIUM: preserve a current receipt for the published child-link table.** Fable identified a concrete evidence gap. The table at `docs/reference/context_buttons.md:228`–238 has the axes of `test_query_plain_child_link_cost` (`tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1075`–1130), including the model/Guardian fixture axis (`:234`). Its `report_measurement` emits a JUnit property and captured stdout (`:1069`–1072). The full combined cost command in `06.49-04-PLAN.md:111` runs this module, but it has neither JUnit output nor uncaptured measurement output; task 04-02 explicitly preserves only the five named F-03 cases plus ancestor slices. Plan 05 nonetheless requires the published figures to trace to plan-04 receipts (`06.49-05-PLAN.md:80`, `:86`). **Needed:** explicitly retain the current `test_query_plain_child_link_cost` measurements in 04-02 (adding legacy JUnit capture to the existing combined run is enough), identify its first-load/fresh-user and runtime conditions, and name that receipt as the source in 05-01. This requires no new fixture, benchmark framework, or broader before-receipt inventory. Source parametrization gives 16 cases total across two providers, not 16 per provider; the raw review's “sixteen-cell-per-provider” suggestion is not adopted as a measured count.
- **N-02 — LOW: make the example and Ruff commands literal executable shell.** `06.49-05-PLAN.md:142` and `:144` contain `&amp;&amp;`. A read-only `bash -n` check of each exact command returns exit 2 at `;&` (see `shell-syntax-receipt.json`); no product command ran. The plan itself says its commands are executable (`:223`), and there is no specified entity-decoding step. **Needed:** replace both separators with literal `&&`, or split each into explicit sequential commands with separate failure checks. Fable's description of silent backgrounding is not the observed Bash result; the grounded defect is invalid literal syntax.

Django does copy `__dict__` while pickling (`django/db/models/base.py:637`–641 in the cached environment), but no repository request loader that restores a cached user was identified. Naming another storage example may help the prose; it adds no missing accepted behavior or distinct task beyond the existing lifetime documentation requirement. No automatic reset or caching support is inferred. |

## Claude Review (fable-review)

# Cross-AI Plan Review — Phase 06.49 (plans 01–05)

**Verdict:** the five plans are sound and stay inside the approved concept, but one task (04-02) cannot pass as written and five smaller gaps need a plan-text fix before execution.

**Basis:** read-only inspection of the repository at `e6394573` and of installed django-guardian 3.5.2 in `.nox/tests-3-12-django-6-0`. I ran no tests and edited nothing; every finding is traced by reading, not by execution.

Plan paths below are abbreviated only in prose; citations use the full path.

---

## 06.49-01

### Summary
Captures real before-figures on unchanged production code: a new list-page cost test plus receipts for the navbar, ancestor and child slices. The fixture choice is correct and will show the effect.

### Strengths
- **The fixture exercises the right path.** `GuardianPublisherListView` declares `cv_list_actions = ["detail", "update", "delete"]` (`tests/test1/app/views.py:716-719`). Each row action calls `cls.cv_has_access(user, obj)` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:941-945`), and each call builds its own checker (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:208-211`).
- **The 0/1/3 row controls are meaningful.** Guardian caches per `(ctype.id, pk)` (`guardian/core.py:170-192`), so cost moves from per-row-per-action to per-row.
- **"Four construction sites" is accurate.** A grep of `src/` finds only `mixins.py:153`, `mixins.py:210`, `ancestors.py:67` and `ancestors.py:70`.
- **Ordering is right.** The baseline is pinned before plan 02 and may not be reconstructed later (`.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-01-PLAN.md:68`).

### Concerns
- **MEDIUM — The named classifier cannot separate checker grant SELECTs from other permission queries.**
  - The plan requires that distinction (`.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-01-PLAN.md:68`) but points the executor at `sql_categories` (`...06.49-01-PLAN.md:65`).
  - That function labels every outer `from "auth_permission"` as `permissions` (`tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1045-1046`).
  - Guardian's checker queries are `Permission.objects.filter(...)` joined to the grant tables (`guardian/core.py:125-149`), so they land in the same bucket as Django's model-permission cache fills and the ViewSet vocabulary read.
  - A "grant SELECTs" pin built on it would silently include unrelated queries.
- **LOW — The before-figures survive only in `.planning/`.** After plan 04 the test holds only after-pins. `tests/test1/test_ancestor_costs.py:51-58` already shows the house pattern of embedding `BASELINE` and `BASELINE_SHA` in the test. Concept D-38 makes this figure the input to the later `prefetch_perms` decision.

### Suggestions
- Use a small local classifier in the new test: a `permissions` query whose SQL joins `guardian_userobjectpermission` or `guardian_groupobjectpermission` (or an enabled direct-FK grant table) is a checker fill; the rest is model or vocabulary work.
- Keep the before-integers and source SHA as a named constant in `test_guardian_checker_costs.py`.
- Scope the "Details present" assertion strictly to the actions cell. `PublisherTable.id` is a `LinkDetailColumn` that emits the same detail URL with no access check (`tests/test1/app/views.py:282-284`, `src/crud_views/lib/table/columns.py:47-50`). The plan already says "row action region"; the trap is just worth naming.

### Risk Assessment
**LOW.** No production change; the one real risk is a mis-attributed pin.

---

## 06.49-02

### Summary
Adds `_get_guardian_checker(user)` and swaps acquisition at the four sites, with construction, isolation, anonymous and decision-independence tests. Small, traceable, no new public surface.

### Strengths
- **The local import keeps the constructor mocks working.** `test_object_branch_result_and_exact_call_order` patches `guardian.core.ObjectPermissionChecker` and uses a `SimpleNamespace` user (`tests/test1/test_access_trace_guardian.py:53-57`), which accepts the attribute. `factory.call_count` at `:69` holds because each case has a new user.
- **The three-answer trace test is unaffected.** One mock checker is built once and `has_perm` is still called three times (`tests/test1/test_access_trace_guardian.py:512-528`).
- **Keeping `_request_checker` as a delegate preserves the one private seam** (`tests/test1/test_access_trace_guardian.py:247`), which only asserts `factory.call_count`.
- **The F-01 exemption is correctly located.** The two comprehensions are at `tests/test1/test_link_access.py:469` and `:476`. The loop at `:479-498` patches the gate, so no checker is involved and it needs no change.

### Concerns
- **MEDIUM — `test_anonymous_direct_hook_reuses_checker` cannot run as described.**
  - The suite sets `ANONYMOUS_USER_NAME=None` (`tests/test1/conftest.py:128`).
  - Guardian copies it into a module constant at import (`guardian/conf/settings.py:7`), so `override_settings` will not help.
  - `get_identity` calls `get_anonymous_user()` unconditionally for an `AnonymousUser` (`guardian/utils.py:129-130`), which runs `user_model.objects.get(username=None)` (`guardian/utils.py:59-63`) and raises `DoesNotExist`.
  - The plan's instruction (`.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-02-PLAN.md:109`) names no settings step.
  - The existing precedent is `monkeypatch.setattr(guardian_settings, "ANONYMOUS_USER_NAME", stored.username)` (`tests/test1/test_delete_databases.py:32-42`).
- **LOW — The expected-red inventory is incomplete.** The plan lists F-01/F-02/F-03/F-05/F-06 as known failures (`...06.49-02-PLAN.md:82`) and omits F-04 (80 navbar cells) and the three new list-cost cases from plan 01. By the probe's breakdown the suite should show exactly 100 pre-existing reds after this plan (2 + 5 + 80 + 9 + 4) plus 3 new ones; anything else is unexpected.
- **LOW — `_request_checker(view, user)` keeps a dead `view` parameter for one test** (`...06.49-02-PLAN.md:66`). Retargeting the single `monkeypatch.setattr` at `tests/test1/test_access_trace_guardian.py:247` would be one line and remove a misleading name. Either choice satisfies D-02.

### Suggestions
- Add to 02-02: patch `guardian.conf.settings.ANONYMOUS_USER_NAME` via `monkeypatch`, create that user, grant on it, then call the hook with `AnonymousUser()`.
- Write the exact expected-red node IDs and counts into `06.49-02-SUMMARY.md` so wave gates compare against a list.
- No change needed for multi-database safety. The cache key ignores the alias (`guardian/core.py:189-192`), but the per-call checker never consulted `obj._state.db` either, so no decision changes.

### Risk Assessment
**LOW–MEDIUM.** Production change is minimal and matches the concept; the risk is one test that fails for configuration reasons.

---

## 06.49-03

### Summary
Proves the D-37 lifetime and adapts F-02/F-05/F-06. The adaptations are narrow and trace correctly against source.

### Strengths
- **The cancel-test adaptation is correct.** The first `cv_get_cancel_url()` at `tests/test1/test_cancel_permissions.py:633` loads an empty Book grant set; the grant at `:635` is invisible to the same user, so `events == [book]` at `:637` needs a fresh actor. `bound()` sets `request.user` directly (`:36-43`), so reassigning it reaches the consumer. The wrong-parent check at `:639-641` still denies.
- **F-06 is naturally request-shaped.** The test issues two real `client.get` calls (`tests/test1/test_ancestor_boundaries.py:542-545`), so distinct users and checkers are real evidence.
- **The local workflow classes are not re-registered.** The metaclass registers only when `cv_viewset` is in the class body (`src/crud_views/lib/view/meta.py:17-24`); `ScopedWorkflow` and `GuardianWorkflow` inherit it (`tests/test1/test_workflow_review.py:212-221`).
- **The workflow follow-up will give 403.** `user_campaign_change` holds only a model-level grant (`tests/test1/conftest.py:759-764`) and the mixin is strict by default.

### Concerns
- **LOW — The temporary URLconf is the most intricate test mechanic in the phase and buys little.** The plan asks for a real Client request to the local class through a bounded test URLconf (`.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-03-PLAN.md:80`). The test already builds its request with `RequestFactory` and an explicit user (`tests/test1/test_workflow_review.py:244-250`). A second request with `User.objects.get(pk=...)` through the same `as_view()` shows the same next-lifetime denial. The real-middleware path is covered separately by `test_next_request_observes_revocation`.
- **LOW — The F-05 list is one site short.** The third grant-then-reuse site is in an F-03 test and is detailed under 06.49-04; the plan's "do not … refetch every tested actor" (`...06.49-03-PLAN.md:102`) must not be read as excluding it.

### Suggestions
- Allow the simpler form for the workflow follow-up: refetched user plus `RequestFactory`, asserting `PermissionDenied`, unchanged state and no `WorkflowInfo`.
- If the URLconf route is kept, state explicitly that `executing` stays `True` and that `loads`/`checked` must be snapshotted first.

### Risk Assessment
**LOW.** Test-only, narrow, and consistent with D-04/D-37.

---

## 06.49-04

### Summary
Reconciles pins from after-measurements. The method is right, but one of the five named child cases cannot pass under the plan's own constraints.

### Strengths
- **Keeping the checker through `cache_conditions` is correct.** That helper deletes only Django's `_perm_cache` family (`tests/test1/test_navbar_costs.py:39-43`).
- **The ancestor file may well stay byte-identical.** `BASELINE` is asserted only for the plain provider (`tests/test1/test_ancestor_costs.py:250-252`). The warm-guard assertion at `:202` still holds because each Client request gets a new user.
- **Before-receipts are immutable and after-values are appended.**

### Concerns
- **HIGH — `test_query_unaffected_core_and_ordinary_actions` has a second failure the probe could not see, and the plan's instructions forbid the fix.**
  - The loop at `tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1335-1339` runs the Guardian parent gate for the `world` actor (`:1324-1325`).
  - That gate goes through the checker (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/ancestors.py:88`), which caches Publisher grants as `["change_publisher"]` (`guardian/core.py:170-187`).
  - Line `:1353` then grants `view` to the same Python user, and `:1358` asserts `links.detail.cv_has_access(actor, parent) is True`.
  - With the shared checker the cached list has no `view_publisher`, so this is `False`.
  - The probe stopped earlier at `:1341` (`permissions > 0` on the second renderer), so this never surfaced.
  - The plan says "do not refetch an actor" (`.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-04-PLAN.md:94`) and requires unchanged visibility assertions (`...06.49-04-PLAN.md:98`). Taken together the task cannot reach "five passed".
- **MEDIUM — New exact pins are verified on Django 6.0 only.** See 06.49-05; the fix can live in either plan.
- **LOW — Two stale comments in a file this plan edits are not named.** "Both positive and negative decisions use a fresh checker each time" (`tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1238`) becomes false for the `repeated` order. The plan names only the navbar comment (`...06.49-04-PLAN.md:73`).

### Suggestions
- Name the third F-05 site in 04-02: re-fetch the actor immediately after `:1353`, use it for both `baseline` and `rendered`, and pin first-load and subsequent values separately. Reword `...06.49-04-PLAN.md:94` to "refetch only where the test changes grants".
- Add the `:1238` comment to the stale-comment sweep.

### Risk Assessment
**MEDIUM** until the HIGH item is written into the plan; **LOW** afterwards.

---

## 06.49-05

### Summary
Publishes lifetime and cost documentation and closes regression evidence. The documentation targets are right, but coverage is narrower than the published surface.

### Strengths
- **F-07 matches a full grep.** The only prose about checker lifetime is `docs/reference/navbar.md:346`, `docs/reference/context_buttons.md:244-246`, `docs/reference/guardian.md:467-468` and `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/ancestors.py:21-23,62-63`.
- **No test pins the old sentences.** A grep of `tests/` for those phrases finds only a code comment.
- **Every named tool and environment exists:** `.nox/tests-3-12-django-{4-2,5-2,6-0}`, `.nox/examples-3-12-django-6-0` with xdist, `.venv/bin/ruff`, `.venv/bin/mkdocs`.
- **Limits are stated honestly:** no installed-distribution or full-matrix claim.

### Concerns
- **MEDIUM — Two more pages repeat the same figures and the same sentence, and are not in `files_modified`.**
  - `docs/reference/card-list-view.md:321-326` and `docs/reference/nested.md:580-584` both restate "8/6/4/2 with a strict Guardian parent" and "repeated Guardian links can still query permissions".
  - Both defer to the context-button table for conditions.
  - The plan lists only three pages (`.planning/phases/06.49-reuse-guardian-object-grants-across-navigation-checks-385/06.49-05-PLAN.md:8-12`) yet rewrites that table (`...06.49-05-PLAN.md:75`).
- **MEDIUM — No CHANGELOG entry is planned.**
  - The plan excludes a Breaking entry (`...06.49-05-PLAN.md:67`) and `CHANGELOG.md` is absent from `files_modified`.
  - `.planning/PROJECT.md:177` says every public-surface change adds an entry.
  - Two behaviours are observable through public hooks: a retained user keeps loaded grants for `cv_has_access(user, obj)`, and the workflow refusal reload re-renders instead of raising.
  - Downstream test suites using the same grant-then-reuse pattern as the package's own F-05 tests will see different answers.
  - Concept 12.3 expects no *Breaking* entry; it does not rule out a plain `### Changed` note.
- **MEDIUM — The four cost modules with new exact pins are never run on Django 4.2 or 5.2.**
  - The 4.2/5.2 selections (`...06.49-05-PLAN.md:113`, `:115`) omit `test_guardian_checker_costs.py`, `test_navbar_costs.py`, `test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py` and `test_ancestor_costs.py`.
  - The project matrix runs all tests on all three series (`CLAUDE.md`, "Development Commands").
  - A whole-response SQL total is the kind of pin most likely to vary by Django version.
  - The published tables are currently labelled Django 5.2.19 (`docs/reference/navbar.md:323`, `docs/reference/context_buttons.md:229`), so a partial update would mix runtimes.
- **LOW — The anonymous wording needs care.** The package's documented setup sets `ANONYMOUS_USER_NAME = None` (`docs/reference/guardian.md:34`), under which a direct anonymous object check raises before and after this phase. The docs should not say direct anonymous hooks "work" without that qualifier.

### Suggestions
- Add `docs/reference/card-list-view.md` and `docs/reference/nested.md` to 05-01, at least to align the "repeated links" sentence and the first-load qualifier.
- Add one `### Changed` entry for #385 covering lifetime, re-fetch guidance and the workflow reload. If the maintainer judges it internal, record that decision in the summary.
- Append the four cost modules to the 4.2 and 5.2 commands. Re-measure each published table wholly on one runtime, or label rows by runtime.

### Risk Assessment
**LOW–MEDIUM.** Nothing here threatens access behaviour; the risks are stale published figures and a CI matrix failure after the phase reports green.

---

## Cross-plan analysis

- **Dependency order is correct.** Measure, then helper, then lifetime, then pins, then docs. Files shared between plans (`test_guardian_checker.py`, `test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py`, `06.49-COST-BASELINE.md`) are only touched in sequential waves.
- **The tree is red by design across waves 2–4.** That is acceptable only with the explicit inventory suggested under 06.49-02.
- **Scope holds.** No plan touches `link_access.py`, the request gate, `get_objects_for_user`, workflow production code, the frozen matrix or goldens. Nothing adds a Django cache, a stored decision, `prefetch_perms` or ambient state. I found no violation of the concept's section 2 anti-goals.
- **D-37 path enumeration matches source.** The workflow reload is the only stock path that re-authorizes the same object after a mid-request grant change (`src/crud_views_workflow/lib/views.py:96-103`).
- **Security.** Checker ownership is per Python user instance, and acquisition stays after the actor, database and fallback branches at all three call sites (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:143-154`, `:197-211`; `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/ancestors.py:82-88`). Section 13's "allowed where it answered denied before" case does occur for a retained user after a revoke, but that is the approved D-09/D-37 lifetime; the remedy is documentation, which is why the CHANGELOG gap matters.
- **Not verified.**
  - Delete and bulk paths beyond what RESEARCH.md cites.
  - Guardian versions below 3.5.2; the declared floor is `django-guardian>=2.4` (`pyproject.toml:53`).
  - The exact figures any measurement will produce.

### Required plan changes, in priority order
1. **04-02:** name `tests/test1/test_guardian_child_link_visibility.py:1353-1358` as a third F-05 refetch site and relax the "do not refetch" wording. (HIGH)
2. **02-02:** add the `guardian_settings.ANONYMOUS_USER_NAME` patch and user creation to the anonymous test. (MEDIUM)
3. **05-02 or 04-02:** run the four cost modules on the cached Django 4.2 and 5.2 environments. (MEDIUM)
4. **05-01:** include `card-list-view.md` and `nested.md`; decide on a `### Changed` CHANGELOG entry. (MEDIUM)
5. **01-01:** use a classifier that isolates Guardian grant-table queries; keep before-figures in the test. (MEDIUM/LOW)
6. **02 summary:** record the exact expected-red inventory. (LOW)

**Overall risk: LOW–MEDIUM.** The design is bounded and matches the approved concept. After item 1 is fixed, the remaining items affect evidence quality and documentation consistency, not access decisions.
